### Black Friday 2026 vira laboratório para compras feitas por IA

### Why macOS Is My Second Favourite OS as a Linux Developer

![Why macOS Is My Second Favourite OS as a Linux Developer](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2F2foidragsgcvmm3dbhlp.jpg>)

### How to install a Claude Code skill: three ways, and what to check first

![How to install a Claude Code skill: three ways, and what to check first](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2F7lpa1t4j4w7bqns983ih.png>)

A skill is a folder with a SKILL.md file. The file starts with a name and a description. Claude Code reads that short description at startup and loads the rest only when the task needs it. Some skills also ship scripts.

*Fontes: [DEV Community](<https://dev.to/tidybytetools/how-to-install-a-claude-code-skill-three-ways-and-what-to-check-first-2gce>)*

### Firecracker vsock: how the host and a microVM talk without a network

![Firecracker vsock: how the host and a microVM talk without a network](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fdsy1pitd0vqmhnya15o8.png>)

If you run code in a microVM, sooner or later you need a channel between the host and the guest that is not the guest's network.

*Fontes: [DEV Community](<https://dev.to/sandscale/firecracker-vsock-how-the-host-and-a-microvm-talk-without-a-network-1nof>)*

### PromptDC: a desktop app that fixes your writing and AI prompts in any app

![PromptDC: a desktop app that fixes your writing and AI prompts in any app](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Ffk98wk0wfc9by1srv8y9.png>)

Most AI writing help lives in one place. Grammar checkers underline words in the browser. AI chats make you copy text in and paste it back. Coding assistants only help with code.

*Fontes: [DEV Community](<https://dev.to/pvisilias/promptdc-a-desktop-app-that-fixes-your-writing-and-ai-prompts-in-any-app-2dcl>)*

### Firecracker rate limiters: token buckets for network, disk and entropy, worked through

![Firecracker rate limiters: token buckets for network, disk and entropy, worked through](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2Fbi8dmswe9sbls7bvcc35.png>)

A microVM that can push unlimited bytes through its network interface or its disk can make every other VM on the host slow. Firecracker's answer is a rate limiter on each emulated device, built from token buckets.

*Fontes: [DEV Community](<https://dev.to/sandscale/firecracker-rate-limiters-token-buckets-for-network-disk-and-entropy-worked-through-4l5k>)*

### Designing Reliable Background Jobs in Python: Retries, Idempotency, and Failure Recovery

Most apps don't do everything inside a single request. Sending an email, processing an upload, generating a report, syncing with another service, crunching a big dataset — all of that takes time.

### Calculate prevailing-wage overtime and bona fide fringe credits in TypeScript
